# Excalidraw Data

## Text Elements
L is the Distance from start to the beginning of cycle

d is the distance from the beginning of cycle to where they first meet

C is the length of the cycle.

Hare travelles twice of tortoise distance

2 * Dtortoise = Dhare

2(L + d) = L + d + nC (It first covered L + d then looped around n times its like a sin(360+x) = sin x  ^HG374puy
